# Spanish 01 — A1 foundations

Conceptual deck: `deck:es-MX:01`. Production: educated contemporary central Mexican
Spanish. One primary actual hidden learning point per source ID; all rows are P.
Text only: sound notes are written planning, not listening or recording evidence.
Guide links pin revision `e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f`.

## Allocation and prerequisites

| Block | IDs | Cards | Objectives | Prerequisites |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- | --- |
| A / es-01-A | 0001–0070 | 70 | Greetings, identity/clarification; five stable vowels, unstressed vowels, stress/syllables, r/rr, j/ñ/ll/y, dental t/d, restrained p/t/k aspiration, linking, question/contrast intonation and spelling distinctions. | None; introductory frames and meanings supplied alongside early targets. |
| B / es-01-B | 0071–0145 | 75 | Articles, noun gender/number, adjective/quantity agreement and useful home/family/food/clothing noun phrases. | A and simple identity frames; row-specific earlier support. |
| C / es-01-C | 0146–0230 | 85 | Ser/estar/hay/tener/llamarse, subject pronouns and reference; identity/state/location/existence/possession and basic useful frames. | A–B; actual earlier frames cited per row. |
| D / es-01-D | 0231–0315 | 85 | Regular present and approximately 25 useful irregular verb lemmas; priority senses, natural complements and collocations. | A–C; agreement and basic noun/reference frames. |
| E / es-01-E | 0316–0380 | 65 | Questions, negation, possessives/demonstratives; person/place/thing, ownership and deixis. | A–D; row-specific earlier reference and verb frames. |
| F / es-01-F | 0381–0450 | 70 | Numbers/dates/time/prices, prepositions and quantity/location frames. | A–E; earlier verb/reference frames. |
| G / es-01-G | 0451–0525 | 75 | Gustar-type patterns, ir a + infinitive, tener que/poder/querer/deber and need/ability/want/obligation distinctions. | A–F; familiar infinitives and ordinary objects. |
| H / es-01-H | 0526–0600 | 75 | Introductory direct objects with visible antecedents; food/services/body/help/social messages and recoverable tú/usted/ustedes. | A–G; no advanced pronoun system. |
| Total | 0001–0600 | 600 | Both §6.1 and §6.2 inventories retained. | No earlier Spanish stage. |

The fixed requirements above come from issue #11 and plan #2; particular lexical
choices and situations are authored expansions, not official examination lists.
Supporting words and introductory frames are described per row; visible exposure
is not credited as another primary taught objective.

## Primary-reference decisions

- [Instituto Cervantes, pronunciation A1–A2](https://cvc.cervantes.es/ensenanza/biblioteca_ele/plan_curricular/niveles/03_pronunciacion_inventario_a1-a2.htm): checked vowel clarity and preservation of unstressed vowel quality, syllable grouping/linking and basic intonation. Use written sound planning in meaningful words; audio verification remains later. No examples copied.
- [RAE/ASALE, México](https://www.rae.es/dpd/M%C3%A9xico): retain recommended x spelling and explain its Spanish j sound in this country name (0025).
- [RAE/ASALE, yeísmo](https://www.rae.es/dpd/ye%C3%ADsmo): ordinary selected ll/y words share the consonant in this production model, with regional realization variation; do not claim one universal narrow realization.
- [RAE/ASALE, agua](https://www.rae.es/dpd/agua): feminine agua takes el immediately before its stressed initial a; adjective agreement remains feminine (0065 and the planned noun-agreement contrast).
- [ASALE, tomate](https://www.asale.org/damer/tomate): use explicit tomate verde in the central-Mexican salsa context (0032), glossed tomatillo. This keeps the intended stable-vowel target and resolves the editor's contextual regional concern. DEM could not be read (HTTP 403); no verification is claimed from it.

- [RAE/ASALE, parecer(se)](https://www.rae.es/dpd/parecer): checked non-pronominal parecer with a noun or adjective complement for appearance; keep this distinct from pronominal parecerse a for resemblance. The assigned beginner inventory uses the non-pronominal appearance frames (0271–0272), with actual naturalness checked by the editor.

- [RAE/ASALE, adónde](https://www.rae.es/dpd/ad%C3%B3nde): independently checked the equally valid spellings adónde / a dónde for destination questions. Card 0320 includes only that explicit orthographic alternative, retaining the interrogative accent; no synonym or accent stripping.

## Complete review and inventory reconciliation

Exactly 600 source cards occupy es-01-0001–es-01-0600, in the eight allocated
blocks above. Each mapping below identifies actual hidden learning, context,
prerequisites and the pinned guide subsection. There are no unresolved findings.

The installed authorized model identifier was `gpt-6.1-sol`, verified from the
local catalog and a successful capability probe, using Codex CLI 0.163.0 and an
existing ChatGPT login. Actual content executions: 15 fresh authors and 15
separate fresh editors, batches 0001–0040 through 0561–0600; one final pass in
0001–0400 and 0401–0600 (two fresh processes). All used high reasoning, ephemeral
sessions, read-only sandbox and structured JSON; no resume, nested agents or
child repository edits. One additional capability probe ran before authoring.
All 33 thread IDs were distinct; zero failed/retried content processes. EXECUTOR
read all complete outputs, resolved findings and alone saved source/coverage.
Raw prompts, outputs and temporary evidence remain in executor-local `/tmp`.

Irregular lemma evidence: ser (es-01-0146); estar (es-01-0161); tener (es-01-0181); haber (es-01-0171); ir (es-01-0241); venir (es-01-0246); hacer (es-01-0248); poder (es-01-0252); querer (es-01-0253); decir (es-01-0018); dar (es-01-0256); ver (es-01-0259); saber (es-01-0261); conocer (es-01-0263); poner (es-01-0265); salir (es-01-0267); traer (es-01-0269); parecer (es-01-0271); entender (es-01-0020); cerrar (es-01-0240); dormir (es-01-0274); pedir (es-01-0275); jugar (es-01-0276); volver (es-01-0277); oír (es-01-0278); doler (es-01-0467); preferir (es-01-0518); pensar (es-01-0520).

The actual rows separately retain sound–spelling selections; article/noun/adjective
agreement; subject reference and ser/estar/hay/tener/llamarse; regular present and
useful irregular forms/complements; questions/negation/ownership/deixis;
number/date/time/price/preposition functions; experiencer preferences and
need/ability/want/obligation/plans; introductory direct objects and practical
lexical/social/service expansion. The complete final pass checked every card and
mapping against both foundation and A1 inventories. These are text judgments,
not native-human review, verified recordings or proficiency certification.

Resolved batch findings included dialogue whitespace, article lemmas, the
lápiz/lápices planning correction, precise contextual support, one duplicate
sentence and inaccurate prerequisite attributions. Verified final-pass findings
and their resolutions are summarized in the PR. Source validation remains
structural evidence; naturalness and substantive coverage are editorial claims
subject to ORCHESTRATOR's complete review. No audio or bundle/import compatibility
is claimed, and human listening is not required in this text phase.
